<div class="featuredEventContainer">
<img class="featuredEventBackground" src="/sites/default/files/pf17_event_bg.png"/>
<div class="featuredEventContent">
<div class="container">
<h2>Find an Event</h2>
<p>Join the Parkinson’s Foundation for one of our fundraising programs. Whether you walk with us at Moving Day®, join the indoor cycling Revolution or fundraise your way with Parkinson’s Champions, we are with you every step of the way.</p>
<div class="featuredEventsBlock">
<div class="featuredEvent">
<p><img alt="" data-entity-type="" data-entity-uuid="" src="/sites/default/files/Images/pf17_moving_day.png" style="max-height:85px"/></p>
<p><span style="border: 0px; font-variant-numeric: inherit; font-weight: 700; font-stretch: inherit; font-size: 18px; line-height: inherit; font-family: MarkOT-Book; vertical-align: baseline; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); text-align: center;">Moving Day® </span><span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-family: MarkOT-Book; font-size: 18px; text-align: center;">is a movement for change — toward more awareness, more funding and a deeper understanding of a disease that affects so many of our family and friends. Are you ready to move?</span></p>
<p class="featurnedEvent__button"><a href="https://movingdaywalk.org/events/" target="_blank">Find a Walk</a></p>
</div>
<div class="featuredEvent">
<p><img alt="" data-entity-type="" data-entity-uuid="" src="/sites/default/files/Images/pf17_parkinsons_champions.png"/></p>
<p><span style="border: 0px; font-variant-numeric: inherit; font-weight: 700; font-stretch: inherit; font-size: 18px; line-height: inherit; font-family: MarkOT-Book; vertical-align: baseline; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); text-align: center;">Parkinson's Champions </span><span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-family: MarkOT-Book; font-size: 18px; text-align: center;">is a way for you to raise funds and awareness for Parkinson's disease any way you like. Take the lead and plan an event or register for an endurance race.</span></p>
<p class="featurnedEvent__button"><a href="/get-involved/Parkinsons-Champions">Start a Fundraiser</a></p>
</div>
<div class="featuredEvent">
<p><img alt="" data-entity-type="" data-entity-uuid="" src="/sites/default/files/2019-10/parkinsons-revolution.png" style="max-height:75px"/></p>
<p><span style="border: 0px; font-variant-numeric: inherit; font-weight: 700; font-stretch: inherit; font-size: 18px; line-height: inherit; font-family: MarkOT-Book; vertical-align: baseline; margin: 0px; padding: 0px; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); text-align: center;">Parkinson’s Revolution </span><span style="color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-family: MarkOT-Book; font-size: 18px; text-align: center;">is an indoor cycling fundraising experience that combines passion, determination and community to advance our mission toward a cure. Join us to perspire and be inspired!</span></p>
<p class="featurnedEvent__button"><a href="https://www.crowdrise.com/parkinsonsrevolution" target="_blank">Join the Revolution</a></p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
